Cada posição carrega a condição em que vale, a garantia que compra, o dissenso vencido e o gatilho que a reabre. ([panlabs-tech/skills](https://github.com/panlabs-tech/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/panlabs-python-standards))

- **panmatch-eval**: Evaluates a unified vision model's ability to perform correspondence matching across stereo disparity estimation, optical flow, and feature matching in a zero-shot setting. It probes cross-domain generalization and robustness to challenging conditions like occlusion, lighting changes, and non-Lambertian surfaces. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Middlebury, ETH3D, KITTI, Infinigen, Spring, Sintel, Booster, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports PCA x. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/panmatch-eval) · ★3)
- **panmind**: Learns your preferences for how an agent should communicate, act, and take risks. Negotiates an update with you when observed behavior drifts from what's declared. ([PanStudio/panmind](https://github.com/PanStudio/panmind/tree/HEAD/panmind-skill) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **panmind-observer**: Goes deeper than a drift report to interpret what PanMind has learned about your preferences. Spawn it to separate stable traits from ones still evolving, explain why drift might be happening, and flag dimensions with too little observation coverage to trust yet. Read-only: it never modifies a profile itself. ([PanStudio/panmind](https://github.com/PanStudio/panmind/tree/HEAD/src/panmind/skills/panmind-observer) · ★2)
- **panna-design**: Use this skill to generate well-branded interfaces and assets for PANNA New Latino Food, either for production or throwaway prototypes/mocks/etc. Contains essential design guidelines, colors, type, fonts, assets, and UI kit components for prototyping. ([cpmappstudio/saborealo](https://github.com/cpmappstudio/saborealo/tree/HEAD/reference/PANNA%20Design%20System))
- **panneau-lateral-chrome-limite-a-certains-onglets**: À utiliser quand une extension Chrome (MV3, API sidePanel) ne doit montrer son panneau latéral que sur certains onglets (un groupe, un site) : « le panneau reste affiché partout », « setOptions enabled:false ne masque rien », « le panneau disparaît quand on change d'onglet dans le groupe », « sidePanel.open may only be called in response to a user gesture ». Dit ce que Chrome fait réellement et l'agencement qui marche. ([haikostudio/beluga](https://github.com/haikostudio/beluga/tree/HEAD/competences/panneau-lateral-chrome-limite-a-certains-onglets))
- **panning-for-gold**: Use when processing voice transcripts, brain dumps, stream-of-consciousness notes, or any raw multi-topic capture. Extracts every idea thread, then evaluates each one with deep brainstorming, then captures results to Open Brain. Trigger on transcripts, exports, "process this", "pan for gold", "brain dump", "what did I say", or multi-topic markdown files. ([NateBJones-Projects/OB1](https://github.com/NateBJones-Projects/OB1/tree/HEAD/skills/panning-for-gold) · ★4.8k · 8 copies)
- **panning-for-gold**: Triage raw unstructured input (transcripts, brain dumps) into evaluated thread inventories and a synthesized gold-found file. Four phases: front-gate, extract, evaluate, synthesize. Use when you say "pan for gold", "triage transcript", "synthesize gold-found", or hand it a voice transcript or brain dump. Do NOT use for structured input like specs or ADRs (use analyst or spec-generator instead). ([rjmurillo/ai-agents](https://github.com/rjmurillo/ai-agents/tree/HEAD/src/claude/skills/panning-for-gold) · ★49 · 6 copies)
- **panno-canvas**: Interact with a Panno infinite canvas via REST API using curl. Use this skill whenever the user wants to create, read, update, or delete shapes on a Panno canvas — including markdown content, geometric shapes, notes, and file editors. Also handles canvas authentication (guest session, API key, OAuth sign-in), layout, responding to comment threads, and the diff/patch workflow for proposing content changes. ([Panno-ai/skills](https://github.com/Panno-ai/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/panno-canvas))
- **pannuke-eval**: Evaluates the ability of deep learning models to perform simultaneous instance segmentation and nuclear classification on histopathology whole-slide image patches across diverse cancer tissue types. Use when the user wants to benchmark on PanNuke, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports mPQ. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pannuke-eval) · ★3)
- **pano**: İkinci beyin panosunu (claude.ai Data artifact) ilk çalışmada kullanıcının kendi claude.ai hesabında yayımlar, sonra vault'un güncel verisiyle yeniler; yalnızca değişen veri setlerini yükler. Vault'ta commit atan her işin sonunda, sohbet sonu hatırlatması istediğinde ve kullanıcı "panoyu güncelle" dediğinde ya da /pano yazdığında kullan. ([AlicanKaya192/Mindgate](https://github.com/AlicanKaya192/Mindgate/tree/HEAD/locales/tr/vault/_system/skills/dashboard) · ★2)
- **pano**: Use when configuring, operating, integrating, troubleshooting, testing, or deploying Pano’s EVM, Bitcoin, or Solana deposit detector, including watches, scanners, normalized deposit events, ingress/egress adapters, RPC behavior, and its internal HTTP API. - **panoptic-radiance-field-eval**: Evaluates a NeRF-based method's ability to jointly reconstruct 3D scene geometry, appearance, and panoptic segmentation (semantic + instance) from multi-view images.
